- **red-team-trinity**: Three always-on adversaries — Security, Scope, Assumptions — that audit the synthesized anneal-alloy plan from three orthogonal angles. Includes 8 numbered Critical Rules and a hard RED Proof Discipline gate: an observed/historical bug, an old CI failure, or a past stack trace is NOT red proof — only a failing test you execute yourself, right now, counts. - **redgreen-loop**: Autonomous build verb of LCD. Use when an LCD work-item (docs/lcd/work/&lt;slug&gt;/ or the conventions artifact-root) has failing tests to drive to green — after Deep-lane test-gen, or once a Standard-lane JOURNAL's STEPS reach failing tests.
